TASK SELECTION: same top-down deferrals as slices 1-3 (idx 23 multi-instance not

FINDING [REAL]: before this slice NO error body carried request_id. The router's
  requestIDMiddleware set only the X-Request-ID RESPONSE header; response.WriteError,
  router writeError, leaderboard writeError, auth writeErrorResponse, the 429
  writers and ~55 handler files that encode bodies themselves all emit
  {"error":{"code","message"}} with no correlation id. chi Recoverer's panic path
  emitted a bare 500 with NO body at all. The main RateLimiter 429 only had the
  Retry-After header, no retry_after_seconds in the body.

DESIGN: one boundary middleware instead of editing ~60 writers.
  A backend/internal/api/middleware/error_envelope.go (202 lines): ErrorEnvelope.
    Buffers ONLY 4xx/5xx bodies (success + SSE stream straight through; Flush is
    delegated, Unwrap exposed for http.ResponseController). On finish:
      - JSON body whose "error" is an object => adds request_id (from the
        X-Request-ID response header) and, on 429/503, retry_after_seconds (from
        Retry-After) — only when absent; all other bytes preserved via
        json.RawMessage (numbers not re-encoded).
      - empty or text/plain body => synthesizes {"error":{code,message,request_id}}
        with a status-derived stable code (BAD_REQUEST/UNAUTHORIZED/FORBIDDEN/
        NOT_FOUND/METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED/CONFLICT/PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE/RATE_LIMITED/
        INTERNAL_ERROR, else upper-snake status text) and the text as message.
      - text/html bodies and JSON whose "error" is a STRING (unsubscribe.go,
        admin.go "error":"CODE" shapes) are left UNTOUCHED — see REMAINING.
      - HEAD errors: status only, no body.
  M backend/internal/api/router.go (+1): r.Use(apimiddleware.ErrorEnvelope) right
    after requestIDMiddleware and OUTSIDE middleware.Recoverer (so panics get an
    envelope). router.go was ALREADY 1233 lines (pre-existing >800 violation);
    this adds one line, no split attempted in this slice.
  A backend/internal/api/middleware/error_envelope_test.go (9 unit tests)
  A backend/internal/api/error_envelope_contract_test.go (router-level contract:
    404 unknown route with generated id, 404 with client-supplied X-Request-ID
    echoed, 405 — each asserts error.code, non-empty message, and
    error.request_id == X-Request-ID header).
  git status --porcelain: M router.go; ?? the 3 new files above. Nothing else.

  A backend/migrations/000101_idempotency_keys.{up,down}.sql: table idempotency_keys
    PK (actor_type, actor_id, operation, idempotency_key), request_hash TEXT, status
    pending|completed, response_status/content_type/body(BYTEA), created_at,
    completed_at; index on created_at. (request_hash first drafted CHAR(64) — the repo
    test caught blank-padding breaking hash equality => changed to TEXT.)
  A backend/internal/models/idempotency.go (29): IdempotencyScope, IdempotencyRecord,
    IdempotencyPending/Completed.
  A backend/internal/db/idempotency.go (104): IdempotencyRepository. Reserve = ONE
    INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE ... WHERE (row older than IdempotencyRetention=24h)
    OR (pending AND older than IdempotencyPendingLease=5m) RETURNING — atomic, one
    winner; otherwise returns the held record. Complete (pending->completed + stored
    response), Release (delete pending).
  A backend/internal/api/middleware/idempotency.go (209): Idempotency(store, op).
    No header / anonymous / nil store => pass-through. Key trimmed, 1-255 chars else
    400 VALIDATION_ERROR. Body read once (MaxBytesError => 413) and restored. Hash =
    sha256(method, path, body) (path included: same key on another post's replies is
    a different request). Reserved => handler runs through a tee recorder; 2xx stored,
    non-2xx or panic => Release (key reusable). Held + hash differs => 409
    IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED; held + pending => 409 IDEMPOTENCY_REQUEST_IN_PROGRESS +
    Retry-After: 1; held + completed => replay stored status/Content-Type/body with
    Idempotent-Replayed: true. Store error => 503 fail-closed (handler never runs
    without the requested dedupe). Record/release use context.WithoutCancel.
  A tests: middleware/idempotency_test.go (12 unit tests, test-only fake store),
    db/idempotency_test.go (5 integration), api/router_idempotency_test.go (1 E2E
    through the real NewRouter). No existing test modified or removed.

"Preserve room delivery and statistics across restarts and more than one API instance"
SLICE 1 of N: cross-instance delivery (steps 1-3) + slow-stream backpressure (part of step 4).

TASK SELECTION: spec.json had an UNCOMMITTED owner edit at iteration start that moved this
  task from idx 78 to idx 23 (above the N-agent acceptance task, now idx 24). I did not
  edit the ledger order; `git add .` commits that owner edit with this slice. First-false
  is now this task, and it is buildable.

DESIGN [REAL, code read]: the DB timeline is the delivery record; notifications are wakeups.
  - migration 000103: AFTER INSERT trigger on room_entries does pg_notify('solvr_room_entries',
    room_id). NOTIFY is sent only at commit (rolled-back entries never announced).
  - hub relay (internal/hub/relay.go): per-room cursor (last fanned-out sequence) set to
    MAX(sequence) when the hub is created (history not re-broadcast). Announce/WakeAll run
    one drain per room (dirty flag, no concurrent drains) that reads ListPage after the
    cursor in pages of RelayPageSize=100 and broadcasts in sequence order; cursor advances
    only past frames handed to the hub; a failed read retries on the next wake.
  - Local writes (submitMessage/submitEvent) call HubManager.Publish: with a relay it just
    wakes the drain (frame re-read from the DB); without one (handler unit tests) it
    broadcasts directly as before. mountRoomRoutes enables the relay on every router.
  - api.StartRoomRelay (main.go, bound to hubCtx): dedicated pgx connection outside the
    pool (application_name solvr-room-relay) LISTENs; each notification -> Announce(room);
    after every (re)LISTEN -> WakeAll (catches lost notifications); 30s sweep -> WakeAll.
  - Backpressure: SSE now uses RoomHub.SubscribeStream; when its 64-slot buffer is full the
    hub CLOSES that stream (removed from registry, sseCount--) instead of silently dropping
    a frame; the SSE handler then writes "retry: 1000" so the client resumes from its
    Last-Event-ID via the existing DB replay. Agent join subscriptions (Subscribe) keep the
    old drop semantics (their channels are never read).

DESIGN:
  - migration 000104: triggers pg_notify('solvr_room_access', room_id), sent at commit, on
    room_members UPDATE of revoked_at/role (when changed) + DELETE; room_agent_tokens
    UPDATE of token_hash/expires_at (when changed) + DELETE; rooms UPDATE of
    is_private/deleted_at/expires_at (when changed) + DELETE. This catches every path
    (handlers, jobs, cascades) without wiring each handler.
  - listener: RoomListener.OnAccess; LISTENs RoomAccessChannel when set.
  - hub/access.go: HubManager.WatchAccess(room) (buffered-1 signal, cancel func),
    AccessChanged(room), AccessChangedAll(). StartRoomRelay: OnAccess -> AccessChanged;
    every (re)LISTEN -> AccessChangedAll (covers notices lost while disconnected).
  - middleware/room_access_recheck.go: RoomAccessRecheck closure left in the request
    context by RoomPolicyGuard (reload room by id; room-token actor: token must still be
    live for the room, via new read-only RoomAgentTokenRepository.IsLive; then the same
    roomActorAllowed) and by BearerGuard (/r adapter: room exists + token live).
  - rooms_sse.go streamRoom: watches access; re-checks once right after watching (closes
    the gap between guard check and watch), on each signal, and on each heartbeat
    (fallback bound = heartbeat, 30s). Denied -> writes `event: access_revoked`
    (data {"code":"ACCESS_REVOKED",...}) and ends, with NO retry directive (a reconnect
    gets 401/403/404 from the guard). A re-check that cannot be read (DB error) keeps the
    stream and logs. New field heartbeatInterval (0 = 30s) for unit tests.

  that expires by the clock alone ends open streams at the next heartbeat recheck (<=30s).
  Homepage all-time/aggregate counts intentionally unchanged (stats, not room content).
  Behavior change: an owner can no longer PATCH a room after it expired (404).

2026-09-25 — Task idx 74 (spec.json, category api) — IN PROGRESS, passes STAYS false
"Standardize errors, pagination, concurrency, and retry behavior across the public API"
SLICE 19 of N. Step 3 invalid-authentication status: optional-auth routes remain anonymous
when credentials are omitted, but reject credentials that are actually presented and invalid.
================================================================================

TASK SELECTION [REAL]: idx 24 remains an acceptance task gated by later multi-instance/client
work; idx 52/53, 68 and 73 remain gated on the canonical knowledge cutover; idx 67 needs a
test deployment. [REAL] No description starts with URGENT. [REAL] The slice-18 journal named
invalid credentials on optional-auth routes as an explicit idx-74 remainder, so this slice
addressed only that contract.

DEFECT [REAL]: OptionalAuthMiddleware treated every authentication failure as anonymous. A
malformed Authorization header, malformed/expired JWT, unknown agent key, unknown human key,
deleted-account JWT, and account-check outage could therefore reach a public handler without
the caller learning that its credential had failed. This contradicted step 3's 401 requirement
for invalid authentication (and the already-established 503 rule for an unavailable account
check). Requests with no Authorization header are still valid anonymous public requests.

TDD RED [TEST]: before implementation,
`GOCACHE=/private/tmp/solvr-go-build-cache-slice19 go test ./internal/auth -run
'^TestOptionalAuthMiddleware_RejectsPresentedInvalidCredentials$' -count=1 -v` executed all
six new cases and exited 1: the anonymous/no-header control passed, while non-Bearer,
malformed JWT, expired JWT, unknown agent-key and unknown user-key cases each got 200 and ran
the next handler instead of returning 401. No case skipped.

IMPLEMENTATION [REAL]: OptionalAuthMiddleware now distinguishes absence from failure. An
absent Authorization header proceeds anonymously. A malformed scheme, invalid/expired JWT or
unknown API key answers 401 with the existing stable auth code; a JWT for a deleted account
answers 401; an account-store failure preserves the existing retryable 503. Valid JWT, agent
key and human key context population is unchanged. The full router's comments now describe
this contract. [REAL, code read; frontend not changed or run] the existing frontend API client
already emits its auth-state event on 401, while the server-rendered public fetches do not send
a bearer token.

EXISTING-TEST REPLACEMENT RECORD (required because this task replaces the old behavior; no
test was deleted, skipped, commented out, renamed away, or loosened):
  - TestOptionalAuthMiddleware previously asserted, verbatim, "handler must ALWAYS be called
    (never 401)" and "status must ALWAYS be 200 (never 401)" for invalid/expired/malformed
    credentials. The same named test remains and now preserves the valid-credential and omitted-
    credential 200 cases while requiring 401 for every presented-invalid case. New differential
    TestOptionalAuthMiddleware_RejectsPresentedInvalidCredentials pins exact stable codes.
  - TestOptionalAuth_JWTOfAGoneAccountIsAnonymous previously asserted optional auth "must
    always continue" and served a gone/uncheckable account anonymously. The historical test
    name remains; it now pins live=200, gone=401 UNAUTHORIZED, unavailable=503
    SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE and proves the downstream handler does not run for either failure.
  - TestSearchEndpoint_WithValidAuth (its key was never registered) previously allowed either
    200 or 401; it now requires 401. TestSearchEndpoint_WithInvalidAuth,

    overrides an explicit Last-Event-ID header, while the comment above ?after= says the header
    wins. Only the accepted forms are documented, not their precedence.

BEHAVIOR CHANGE (v0) [REAL]:
  - A per-agent room token sent to any optional-auth route (room routes, /v1/search, /v1/posts,
    ...) is no longer 401 INVALID_API_KEY. On room routes RoomPolicyGuard judges it (401
    UNAUTHORIZED unknown/expired, 403 another room); elsewhere the caller is anonymous.
    Mandatory-auth routes still reject a room token (401).
  - Trade-off, OBSERVED: a garbage solvr_rt_... on a non-room optional route (search, posts)
    is anonymous 200, because nothing there can validate a room token; this is the pre-slice-19
    behavior for that prefix only.
  - DEPLOY WARNING [REAL]: nothing here is deployed. Slices 19-21 must NOT ship without this
    commit, or every room agent gets 401.

  /members, /messages) answered INVALID_JSON; posts/replies/comments/votes/save-as-post answered
  VALIDATION_ERROR. Slice 20 also saw INVALID_REQUEST (auth, agents/claim) and BAD_REQUEST
  (PATCH /v1/me).

  no test, no frontend, cli, mcp-server or SDK file and no ~/.claude/skills/solvr script reads
  INVALID_JSON, INVALID_REQUEST, or BAD_REQUEST (only .planning/.../03-01-PLAN.md mentions them).
  VALIDATION_ERROR is the code of 246 StatusBadRequest sites (heuristic grep) vs 24 malformed-body
  sites for the other codes (19 changed here + 5 admin), so it has the smallest blast radius.

DECISION [mine, v0 = no backwards compat]: a body that is not valid JSON is 400 VALIDATION_ERROR
on every public route. The 5 admin-surface writers (handlers/admin.go x2, incidents_admin.go x3,
INVALID_JSON) are operator routes, not the public API, and are NOT changed.

SPIKE FIRST [REAL, MEASURED] (throwaway backend/internal/api/zz_spike25_test.go, deleted before
  the commit; log /tmp/solvr_idx75_s1_spike.log; DATABASE_URL=postgres://solvr:solvr_dev@
  localhost:5435/solvr_test, the local docker solvr-postgres): one agent, two sessions on one
  room. Session B handshakes (the connect prompt tells every session to). Result:
  - tokA != tokB; room_agent_tokens holds ONE row per (room, agent) (PRIMARY KEY (room_id,
    agent_id); Issue = INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE SET token_hash).
  - session A: GET /v1/rooms/{slug}/entries 401 UNAUTHORIZED "invalid or expired room token";
    POST /r/{slug}/message 401 the same; identical to the answer a garbage token gets.
  - session A's already-open stream ended with event access_revoked (wrong cause: access was
    never revoked, the credential was replaced).
  - ping-pong: A recovers by handshaking again -> B's token is dead (401), A(new) 200.

DESIGN [mine, v0 = no backwards compat]:
  - A handshake ADDS a session token; the agent's other tokens keep working.
  - {"rotate": true} is the explicit replacement: in one transaction (advisory lock per
    (room, agent)) every live token is marked rotated_at = NOW() and a new one is inserted.
  - A rotated token is answered 401 CREDENTIAL_ROTATED (message names POST
    /v1/rooms/{slug}/handshake) on RoomPolicyGuard routes and BearerGuard (/r) routes, and its
    open streams end with event credential_rotated (not access_revoked). A token that never
    existed, expired or was revoked stays 401 UNAUTHORIZED: revoke DELETES every row of the
    pair (tombstones included), so an owner action is never disguised as "just handshake again".
  - Bound: MaxLiveRoomAgentTokens = 10 live tokens per (room, agent); one more is 409
    TOKEN_LIMIT_REACHED (message names rotate), never a silent eviction. Rotation is never
    refused for the limit. MaxRotatedRoomAgentTokens = 20 tombstones per pair, none older than
    30 days, purged inside Rotate.
  - Handshake 201 gains data.rotated (true when earlier live tokens were replaced).

BEHAVIOR CHANGE (v0) [REAL]:
  - POST /v1/rooms/{slug}/handshake no longer replaces the agent's earlier token: it adds one.
    An external client that relied on "a new handshake kills the old token" as a way to revoke
    a leaked token must now send {"rotate": true} (or ask the owner to revoke).
  - New codes: 401 CREDENTIAL_ROTATED (replaced token), 409 TOKEN_LIMIT_REACHED (11th live token),
    stream event credential_rotated. A stream whose token was replaced no longer says
    access_revoked.
  - Sessions accumulate until rotated: tokens without ttl_seconds never expire, so an agent that
    handshakes on every start and never rotates hits the limit after 10 sessions and gets
    409 TOKEN_LIMIT_REACHED with the way out named in the message.

  from earlier slices) on production via the admin query route, then deploys immediately.

WHAT SLICE 2 DOES [REAL, read from the code]:
  - auth/stream_ticket.go: IssueStreamTicket / ParseStreamTicket. Stateless HMAC-SHA256 ticket,
    prefix solvr_st_ (none of the bearer prefixes, not a JWT), TTL 60 s (StreamTicketTTL), key
    derived from the JWT secret with its own domain string so it is not the JWT key. Claims: room
    id, credential kind (human | agent_api_key | room_token), subject, admin flag, and for a room
    token only the STORED HASH (no secret inside the ticket). Errors ErrStreamTicketInvalid /
    ErrStreamTicketExpired.
  - handlers/rooms_stream_ticket.go: POST /v1/rooms/{slug}/stream-ticket behind the room READ
    policy (credential in the Authorization header) and httprate 30 per minute per IP; 201 with
    ticket, expires_at, ttl_seconds, stream; Cache-Control no-store; anonymous is 401.
  - middleware/stream_ticket.go: SSEStreamTicket wired ONLY on GET /v1/rooms/{slug}/stream. Reads
    ?ticket= only when there is no Authorization header; ?access_token= and ?token= are refused
    with 400 VALIDATION_ERROR that names the replacement and never echoes the value; expired /
    forged ticket is 401 STREAM_TICKET_EXPIRED / STREAM_TICKET_INVALID (recoverable: mint again).
    middleware/sse_access_token.go (the BART-156 query-to-header promotion) is DELETED.
  - middleware/room_policy.go streamTicketActor: the ticket is bound to one room (another room is
    403); a room-token ticket is re-resolved through the token table at open time, so a token
    revoked or rotated after minting opens nothing (rotation answers CREDENTIAL_ROTATED via
    tokenMiss); room_access_recheck.go uses RoomTokenHash(r) so the 30 s recheck of an OPEN stream
    checks the stored hash carried in the ticket. handlers/rooms_sse.go allows ?ticket= instead of
    ?access_token= in the stream's query allowlist.
  - middleware/logging.go: "ticket" added to the redacted query parameters.
  - OpenAPI: operation createRoomStreamTicket, StreamTicketResponse, StreamTicket parameter
    (replaces StreamAccessToken), x-solvr-conventions credential_transport.stream_query_parameter
    now name ticket + mint_route + ttl_seconds, shared 401 row names the two ticket codes.
  - Frontend: api.createRoomStreamTicket(slug); hooks/use-room-sse.ts mints a ticket for every
    (re)connect when a JWT is in localStorage and opens EventSource with ?ticket=; if minting
    fails it retries instead of opening an unauthenticated stream; unmount before the ticket
    arrives opens nothing. Anonymous viewers open the stream at once with no ticket.
  - Agent docs: frontend/public/references/api.md and skill/references/api.md describe the ticket.

WHAT 858f450 DOES [REAL, read from the diff]:
  - migration 000106_room_access_account_deletion.{up,down}.sql: function account_deleted_access_
    notify() and triggers users_deleted_access_notify / agents_deleted_access_notify (AFTER UPDATE
    OF deleted_at, only on NULL -> not NULL). They pg_notify the solvr_room_access channel (000104's)
    with the id of every room where the account holds an active membership, so open streams on
    EVERY API instance re-check in about a second instead of at the 30 s heartbeat.
  - db/room_agent_tokens.go: liveAgentToken = liveToken AND the agent's agents row has deleted_at
    IS NULL; ResolveByHash and IsLive use it. A deleted agent's per-agent room token stops
    resolving everywhere (REST, /r adapter, tickets, open streams) without sweeping token rows, and
    an admin recovery restores it. A deletion is NOT a rotation (401 UNAUTHORIZED, not CREDENTIAL_
    ROTATED).
  - db/room_members.go AccountLive(actorType, id): human -> users.deleted_at IS NULL, agent ->
    agents.deleted_at IS NULL, unknown type or malformed id -> not live.
  - middleware/room_access_recheck.go accountStillLive + policyRecheck: every recheck of an open
    stream (except a room-token actor, which the token repository already covers) asks whether the
    account still exists. middleware/room_policy.go resolveRoomActor (new memberRepo parameter,
    callers updated in room_access_guard.go and RoomPolicyGuard): a stream TICKET minted before the
    deletion is refused (errRoomAccountGone -> 401) because no account middleware saw it.
  - middleware/logging.go: request-body redaction is no longer "JSON object, field named exactly
    password/token". Field names are matched by fragment (password, passwd, apikey, token, secret,
    credential, ticket, authorization, privatekey; exact `key`) blind to _ - . and case; arrays
    and nested arrays are walked; a body that is not a JSON object/array (malformed, truncated,
    form-encoded, single-quoted) goes through secretTextPattern, which keeps the name and replaces
    the value. This CORRECTS slice 3's NOT VERIFIED note that said the body redactor "has no
    ticket, claim_token or key, and returns a non-object body unchanged": that gap is closed.
  - Docs: frontend/public/{skill.md, references/api.md, references/examples.md} stop describing the
    shared solvr_rm_ token, /rooms/:slug/rotate-token and the handshake `room_token` body field;
    api.md documents presence as the authenticated member's own row (AGENT_NAME_TAKEN 409, 403 for
    a non-member) [REAL, read] which matches handlers/rooms_presence.go (JoinRoom returns 409
    AGENT_NAME_TAKEN, `agent_id` is in models/agent_presence.go).
  - Tests (all new): middleware/logging_body_secrets_test.go, db/room_account_deletion_test.go,
    db/room_members_account_live_test.go, api/router_rooms_deleted_account_streams_test.go (two API
    instances), api/published_docs_room_credentials_test.go.

WHAT THIS SLICE DOES [REAL, `git status --porcelain` / `git diff --stat` before this append: 9
  files modified (91 insertions, 76 deletions), 10 new files]:
  Design: the callback redirects with ?code=<solvr_lc_...>, a single-use 60-second code. The
  frontend POSTs it to POST /v1/auth/oauth/exchange {"login_code": ...} and receives
  {data:{access_token, token_type:"Bearer", expires_in}} in the response body (Cache-Control:
  no-store). The JWT is minted at exchange time from the redeemed account. Only the SHA-256 of a
  code is stored; nothing is held in process memory; two API instances share the table.
  Production (backend):
  N migrations/000107_oauth_login_codes.{up,down}.sql: table oauth_login_codes(id, code_hash
    CHAR(64) UNIQUE, user_id -> users ON DELETE CASCADE, expires_at, consumed_at, created_at) and an
    index on expires_at.
  N db/oauth_login_codes.go: OAuthLoginCodeRepository.Issue (generates the code, deletes codes that
    expired more than an hour ago, inserts hash + DB-clock expiry) and Redeem (one CTE: UPDATE ...
    SET consumed_at WHERE code_hash AND consumed_at IS NULL AND expires_at > NOW() RETURNING user_id,
    joined to users with deleted_at IS NULL). ErrLoginCodeInvalid for unknown, used, expired or
    deleted-account codes (indistinguishable to the caller). OAuthLoginUser{ID, Email, Role}.
  M token/token.go: GenerateLoginCode (prefix solvr_lc_, 256 bits; not a bearer prefix, not a JWT).
  N auth/login_code.go: LoginCodeTTL = 60 s.
  N handlers/oauth_login_code.go: OAuthLoginCodeStore interface, OAuthHandlers.WithLoginCodes,
    redirectWithLoginCode (500 INTERNAL_ERROR, no Location, when there is no store or Issue fails:
    it never falls back to a token in the URL), ExchangeLoginCode (4 KiB body cap; 400
    VALIDATION_ERROR for bad JSON / missing login_code; 401 INVALID_LOGIN_CODE; 500 on a store
    failure).
  M handlers/oauth.go (9 insertions, 44 deletions): OAuthHandlers gets a loginCodes field; the
    "Generate JWT" step is gone from both callbacks (steps renumbered), and the redirect is
    h.redirectWithLoginCode(w, r, user.ID). The refresh-token step is untouched.
  M router.go: the OAuth handlers get db.NewOAuthLoginCodeRepository(pool); route
    r.With(BlockAgentAPIKeys).Post("/auth/oauth/exchange", ...). M route_families.go: the route joins
    the "auth" family (keep). M middleware/logging.go: body fields containing "logincode" (and
    login_code text pairs) are redacted, since a FAILED exchange logs its request body.
  Frontend: M app/auth/callback/page.tsx: reads ?code= (not ?token=), calls history.replaceState to
    strip it before any request, POSTs to ${NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL}/v1/auth/oauth/exchange, then
    setToken(access_token) and the unchanged referral claim / return-URL logic. A startedRef guard
    stops the effect from exchanging twice (React StrictMode runs effects twice in dev, and
    replaceState changes the search params, which re-runs the effect). A URL that still carries
    ?token= is refused ("No login code received"): it is not exchanged, stored or trusted.
  Docs: M backend/docs/oauth-setup.md (the browser flow and the "verify the token" curl now
    describe the code + exchange). SPEC.md NOT edited (idx 75's steps do not name it).
  Tests (new): db/oauth_login_codes_test.go (6), handlers/oauth_login_code_test.go (3 callback
    tests over GitHub and Google mock providers, 3 exchange tests), api/router_oauth_login_code_test.go
    (5, real router + Postgres, two router instances), frontend/app/auth/callback/page.test.tsx (7).

WHAT THIS SLICE DOES [REAL, `git status --porcelain` / `git diff --numstat` before this append:
  22 tracked files changed besides progress.txt (2 deleted, 1 added-staged), 9 untracked new]:
  Design: claim_url is {base}/claim#token=<token>. A fragment is never sent to a server. The page
  reads it, stores it in sessionStorage (survives the login round trip, never sent in a request),
  replaceState's it out of the address bar, and asks the API with POST /v1/agents/claim/lookup
  {"token": ...}; claiming stays POST /v1/agents/claim (already a body). Storage: a row keeps
  token_hash = sha256 hex (lookup) and token_sealed = AES-256-GCM(token) under
  HMAC-SHA256(JWT_SECRET, "solvr/claim-token-seal/v1"), with the row's hash as additional data, so
  a repeat POST /v1/agents/me/claim still returns the SAME live link and no table holds the token.
  A live row whose seal cannot be opened (pre-000108 rows, or a changed JWT_SECRET) is deleted
  and replaced with a new token instead of returning a blank link.
  Backend production:
  N migrations/000108_claim_token_hash.{up,down}.sql: add token_hash (backfilled with
    encode(sha256(convert_to(token,'UTF8')),'hex'), NOT NULL, UNIQUE) + token_sealed BYTEA, drop
    idx_claim_tokens_token and the token column. Down re-adds token filled with token_hash (the
    clear tokens are gone for good; old links stop working after a down).
  N db/claim_token_seal.go (68): hashClaimToken, claimTokenSealer (seal/open; nil sealer = no key).
  M db/claim_tokens.go: WithSealSecret; Create inserts hash + seal; FindByToken matches token_hash
    and returns the presented value as Token; FindActiveByAgentID opens the seal (Token "" when it
    cannot); new DeleteUnusedByAgentID (used rows kept: they record who claimed).
  M models/claim_token.go: comment only (what Token now means).
  M handlers/agents.go: ClaimTokenRepositoryInterface gains DeleteUnusedByAgentID.
  M handlers/agents_claim.go: claimURL() builds {baseURL}/claim#token=; GenerateClaim replaces an
    unopenable live token; GetClaimInfo (chi path param) -> LookupClaim (JSON body, 4 KiB cap,
    400 VALIDATION_ERROR bad body, 400 MISSING_TOKEN empty token; the rest unchanged: 200 with

  M router.go: claimTokenRepoConcrete.WithSealSecret(jwtSecret); r.Get("/claim/{token}") ->

    ExtractStaticSQL(root) parses every non-test .go file (vendor/node_modules/dot dirs
    skipped, same walk as ScanLegacySourceDependencies) with go/parser and returns, sorted by
    file+line, every string that folds at compile time (literal, raw string, + concatenation,
    package constants incl. ones declared in another file of the same directory, resolved to
    a fixpoint) and starts with an upper-case SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE/WITH + whitespace.
    A concatenation with a runtime value yields its constant parts; fmt verbs stay in the text.
    A bare constant identifier is recorded at its declaration, not at each use. An unparsable
    file is an error naming the file.

DECISION (canonical eligibility, recorded in the registry note): a candidate is a live post of
  ANY type that is public + published + approved, has no crystallization_cid, was last
  updated more than 7 days ago, has at least one live human or agent reply, and has no live
  human/agent reply updated inside those 7 days. Longest-stable first, 50 per run (unchanged
  limit). Snapshot format "2.0" = post (title, body, tags, votes, author, created/updated) +
  every live human/agent reply (id, parent_reply_id, body, votes, legacy_type, author,
  created_at), system verdicts left out. Existing v1.0 CIDs are untouched.

    outcomes": posts.source_room_id marks a post saved from a room (FindPublishedBySourceRoom).
  - The legacy platform queries filter on status only: pulse, trending (status draft/closed)
    and /me/diff counted family/draft/pending posts too.
  - Legacy PlatformBriefingRepository / RecommendationRepository / InferredSpecialties tests
    (briefing_platform_test.go, briefing_rising_ideas_test.go, briefing_recommendations_test.go,
    inferred_specialties_test.go) pin the legacy behavior, so the canonical code is new
    repositories (slice 11 pattern) and the legacy ones stay, unwired, until the tables drop.
    BriefingDiffRepository has no DB test (handler tests use mocks), so its opportunity query
    changed in place.

DECISIONS (recorded in the feature:briefing registry note):
  public post = live, visibility public, published, approved. contributor reply = live, author
  not 'system' (liveContributorReply from slice 11).
  - pulse: open_posts (NEW json field) = public posts with status open/in_progress/active, any
    type; open_problems/open_questions/active_ideas = the problem/question/idea subsets of that
    (type passed as a parameter; they read 0 once posts_type_check narrows);
    new_posts_last_24h and solved_last_7d (status 'solved', updated in 7d) over public posts
    only; contributors_this_week = distinct (type,id) authors of this week's public posts and
    contributor replies on public posts. Active agents and blog count unchanged.
  - trending: same velocity (confirmed post votes + views in 7d), public posts not closed,
    own-post exclusion by (agent, id).
  - rising ideas: public posts of ANY type not dormant/closed with a contributor reply or an
    upvote, ranked by contributor replies, upvotes, recency; evolved_count always 0.
  - hardcore: public posts not solved/answered/closed/evolved with 3+ contributor replies, or
    older than 30 days with a positive score; difficulty (1+replies)*ln(age_days+2)*
    (1+max(score,0)*0.5); weight always 1, failed_count always 0 (retired).
  - recent victories: public posts saved from a room (source_room_id) in the last 14 days,
    newest first; solver = post author (display name, fallback id), total_approaches =
    contributor replies, days_to_solve = whole days room.created_at -> post.created_at (0 if
    the room row is gone), solved_at = post created_at in UTC. Legacy solved-problem victories
    no longer appear.
  - you might like: same two steps; interacted = the agent's post votes, its votes on a
    post's replies, its live replies; candidates public and not closed; co-occurring tags
    learned from public posts only; voter_type 'agent' required.
  - inferred specialties (agent): posts written 2, live replies on live posts 2, confirmed
    post upvotes 1 (legacy: approaches 2, answers 1, comments/responses 0).
  - /me/diff new_opportunities = briefingOpportunityWhere (same rule as the briefing's
    opportunities, every post type) + created after since. CountTrendingSince, CountNewSince
    and ListAwardedSince unchanged (no legacy dependency).

OBSERVED, NOT CHANGED [REAL, code read + mut2 log]: badges.description is nullable
  (000051 `description TEXT`). BadgeRepository.ListForOwner scans it into a string without
  COALESCE, so a badge row with NULL description makes GET /v1/{agents,users}/{id}/badges
  return 500. Award always writes a string, so rows it writes are safe. A row inserted another
  way (for example through the admin query route) would break that owner's badge list.
  Owner's call; not in this task's scope.

BEHAVIOR CHANGES THE OWNER SHOULD KNOW [REAL, code read + tests above, unless stated]:
  - DEPLOY ORDER. The served leaderboard now reads reputation_history, and only the cutover
    fills it (RemapLegacyRelations -> FreezeLegacyReputation). No command wires the cutover:
    it has no production caller [REAL, grep] and its runner belongs to idx 93. Deploying this
    code without 000111 makes both leaderboard routes fail on the missing relation
    [UNVERIFIED, inferred from the SQL]. Deploying with 000111 but BEFORE the freeze runs
    makes the leaderboard show only the agent bonus plus votes on posts: every problem, idea,
    answer, response and comment point and every answer/response vote point disappears from
    the board until the freeze runs. mut2 shows the board differs without it [TEST]. The
    freeze is idempotent and can run on its own before the rest of the cutover.
  - Legacy writes that keep coming in after the freeze (legacy routes stay live during the
    transition) are not scored until the freeze runs again.
  - After the cutover nothing scores for writing: posts and replies earn 0, and so does
    solving (no status workflow). Only confirmed votes on posts and replies score (+2/-1).
    Before, a problem earned 25, an idea 15, an answer 10, a response 5 and a comment 2.
  - GAP: the profile and list reputation surfaces still use the legacy formulas:
    GetAgentStats, GetUserStats, the agents list (agents.go:925-950) and the users list
    (BuildReputationSQL). After the cutover they will disagree with the served leaderboard.
    TestReputation_CrossEndpointConsistency compares GetUserStats and the users list against
    the LEGACY LeaderboardRepository (reputation_integration_test.go:30), not the served
    board, so nothing guards that agreement right now. This is feature:reputation, the next
    slice.
  - The legacy formulas already disagree with each other [REAL, code read]: agent stats and
    the agents list do not score comments; BuildReputationSQL does. The frozen history uses
    BuildReputationSQL's events, comments included.

  - DEPLOY ORDER. The served statistics read ONLY replies (plus posts/agents/users). Until the

    posts.accepted_answer_id holds answer ids until RemapAcceptedAnswerReferences.

    answers, approaches and responses); replies on deleted posts no longer count (the legacy
    count included approaches on a deleted problem); moderation verdicts never count.

    on the question now counts), not the first answer.
